---

[dict.2.abandon]
To abandon something is to leave it and not come back to it.

[dict.2.abbreviate]
To abbreviate a word is to make it shorter, such as by writing only some of its letters.

[dict.2.ability]
Ability is being able to do a thing.

[dict.2.absolute]
Absolute means all of a thing and not only part of it.

[dict.2.absolutely]
Absolutely means with nothing left out.

[dict.2.absorb]
To absorb a thing is to take it in.

[dict.2.abundant]
Abundant means found in large amounts.

[dict.2.academy]
An academy is a school.

[dict.2.access]
Access is a way to get to a thing or into a place.

[dict.2.accessible]
Accessible means easy to reach, get or use.

[dict.2.accident]
An accident is a bad thing that happens when nobody wanted it to.

[dict.2.according]
According means as was said, or in a way that matches.

[dict.2.account]
What someone says happened, told in order.

[dict.2.accumulation]
Accumulation is the slow building up of something over time.

[dict.2.accuracy]
Accuracy is being right and not off by much.

[dict.2.accurate]
Accurate means exactly right, with no mistakes.

[dict.2.accurately]
Accurately means in a way that is correct and exact.

[dict.2.accustomed]
To be accustomed to something is to be used to it because it has happened often.

[dict.2.acid]
An acid is a substance that eats into some things it touches.

[dict.2.acidic]
Acidic means of an acid or like an acid.

[dict.2.acidity]
Acidity is how much acid something has in it.

[dict.2.acre]
An acre is a unit for measuring how much land there is.

[dict.2.act]
To act is to do something, and an act is one thing that someone does.

[dict.2.action]
An action is something that someone does.

[dict.2.active]
Active means doing something.

[dict.2.activity]
An activity is something that people do, often for fun or to learn.

[dict.2.actual]
Actual means real.

[dict.2.actually]
Actually means really, or in fact.

[dict.2.add]
To add is to put one amount with another to make a bigger amount.

[dict.2.additional]
Additional means added, or more than there was before.

[dict.2.adequate]
Adequate means enough for what is needed.

[dict.2.adjacent]
Adjacent means next to something.

[dict.2.administrative]
Administrative means about the work of running a group.

[dict.2.adult]
An adult is a person who is not a child.

[dict.2.advanced]
Advanced means more developed or more modern than others of its kind.

[dict.2.advantage]
An advantage is something that helps you do better.

[dict.2.adverse]
Adverse means harmful, or working against you.

[dict.2.advise]
To advise someone is to tell them what you think they should do.

[dict.2.advisory]
An advisory is a report that tells people about a danger, such as a coming storm, and what they should do.

[dict.2.aerial]
Aerial means taken from or done in the air, such as a photograph taken from an airplane.

[dict.2.aerosol]
An aerosol is a very small bit of a thing in the air.

[dict.2.affect]
To affect something is to change it or to act on it.

[dict.2.affection]
Affection is a warm feeling for a person.

[dict.2.afternoon]
Afternoon is the part of the day after the middle of the day.

[dict.2.afterward]
Afterward means at a later time.

[dict.2.age]
Age is how long a thing has been alive.

[dict.2.agency]
An agency is a group that does a kind of work for people.

[dict.2.ago]
Ago means before now, as in two days ago.

[dict.2.agricultural]
Agricultural means of farms and of growing food.

[dict.2.agriculture]
Agriculture is the work of growing crops and raising animals for food.

[dict.2.aircraft]
An aircraft is a thing that flies in the air.

[dict.2.airflow]
Airflow is air moving from one place to another.

[dict.2.airplane]
An airplane is a machine with wings that flies through the air and carries people or things.

[dict.2.airport]
An airport is a place where airplanes take off and land.

[dict.2.alarm]
An alarm is a sound that says something bad is happening.

[dict.2.alcohol]
Alcohol is the name of a group of clear liquids that burn, and one kind of it is in some drinks.

[dict.2.alert]
Alert means ready and watching.

[dict.2.algae]
Algae are very small plants that live in water.

[dict.2.algal]
Algal means of algae.

[dict.2.algorithm]
An algorithm is a list of steps for doing a thing.

[dict.2.align]
To align things is to put them in a straight line or facing the same way.

[dict.2.alleviate]
To alleviate something bad is to make it less bad.

[dict.2.almost]
Almost means very close to all of something, but not all of it.

[dict.2.aloft]
Aloft means up in the air.

[dict.2.alongside]
Alongside means next to, or along the side of.

[dict.2.aloud]
Aloud means in a voice that others can hear.

[dict.2.already]
Already means before now.

[dict.2.alter]
To alter something is to change it.

[dict.2.alteration]
An alteration is a change made to something.

[dict.2.alternative]
An alternative is another thing that you can choose in place of the first one.

[dict.2.although]
Although means even though, as in we went out although it was cold.

[dict.2.altitude]
Altitude is how high something is above the ground.

[dict.2.altocumulus]
Altocumulus is cloud at middle height in small piles or rolls.

[dict.2.altogether]
Altogether means all of them counted as one.

[dict.2.altostratus]
Altostratus is a gray sheet of cloud at middle height.

[dict.2.aluminum]
Aluminum is a light, silver-white metal used to make foil and airplanes.

[dict.2.amazing]
Amazing means so surprising or so good that it fills you with wonder.

[dict.2.amount]
An amount is how much there is of a thing.

[dict.2.ample]
Ample means more than enough.

[dict.2.analysis]
An analysis is a careful look at the parts of a thing.

[dict.2.analyze]
To analyze something is to study its parts closely to find out what it is made of or how it works.

[dict.2.ancient]
Ancient means very old, or from a time long ago.

[dict.2.angel]
An angel is a spirit that some people believe in, often shown as a person with wings.

[dict.2.angle]
An angle is the corner made where two straight lines meet.

[dict.2.announce]
To announce something is to tell many people about it.

[dict.2.annual]
Annual means once in a year.

[dict.2.annually]
Annually means once each year.

[dict.2.antenna]
An antenna is a long, thin piece of metal that sends or receives sound and pictures through the air.

[dict.2.anvil]
An anvil is a heavy metal block that a person hits hot metal on.

[dict.2.anxious]
Anxious means worried and afraid about what may happen.

[dict.2.anybody]
Anybody means any person at all.

[dict.2.anyway]
Anyway is a word you use to go back to the main point, or to say that something is true all the same.

[dict.2.anywhere]
Anywhere means in any place.

[dict.2.apartment]
An apartment is a home that is part of a larger building.

[dict.2.apparent]
Apparent means looking real, whether it is or not.

[dict.2.apparently]
Apparently means as it seems, from what can be seen or heard.

[dict.2.appearance]
The appearance of something is the way it looks.

[dict.2.appliance]
An appliance is a machine that does a job in the home.

[dict.2.application]
An application is a way of using a thing to do a job.

[dict.2.apply]
To apply a thing is to put it on something.

[dict.2.approach]
To approach something is to move nearer to it.

[dict.2.appropriate]
Appropriate means right for the time and the place.

[dict.2.approximate]
Approximate means close but not exact.

[dict.2.approximately]
Approximately means close to, but not exactly.

[dict.2.aquatic]
Aquatic means living in water.

[dict.2.aqueduct]
An aqueduct is a channel or a bridge built to carry water over a long distance.

[dict.2.area]
An area is a part of a place or of a surface.

[dict.2.arid]
Arid means very dry, with very little rain.

[dict.2.array]
An array is a set of things put together in lines.

[dict.2.arrive]
To arrive is to get to a place.

[dict.2.arrow]
An arrow is a thin stick with a sharp point at its front end, or a mark shaped like one that points the way.

[dict.2.art]
Art is a picture or other made thing that a person makes to be looked at.

[dict.2.artichoke]
An artichoke is a green vegetable made of thick leaves that grow in a tight ball.

[dict.2.artificial]
Artificial means made by people, not by nature.

[dict.2.artificially]
Artificially means in a way that is made or caused by people, not by nature.

[dict.2.artwork]
An artwork is one piece of art.

[dict.2.ascend]
To ascend is to go up.

[dict.2.ash]
Ash is the gray dust left after a fire.

[dict.2.ashamed]
Ashamed means feeling bad about something you did.

[dict.2.ashore]
Ashore means onto or on the land from the water.

[dict.2.aside]
Aside means to one side, or out of the way.

[dict.2.asphalt]
Asphalt is a black, sticky material used to make roads.

[dict.2.assemble]
To assemble is to come together in one place, or to put parts together.

[dict.2.assess]
To assess something is to judge how good, how large or how important it is.

[dict.2.assist]
To assist someone is to help them.

[dict.2.assistance]
Assistance is help.

[dict.2.assume]
To assume a thing is to take it as true without checking.

[dict.2.assure]
To assure someone is to tell them something in a strong way so that they stop worrying.

[dict.2.asteroid]
An asteroid is a rock in space that goes around the sun.

[dict.2.astonish]
To astonish someone is to surprise them very much.

[dict.2.astrology]
Astrology is the idea that the places of the sun, the moon and the planets in the sky can tell about the lives of people. Most scientists do not accept it, because its claims have not been shown by evidence.

[dict.2.astronaut]
An astronaut is a person who travels in space.

[dict.2.astronomer]
An astronomer is a scientist who studies the stars, the planets and other things in space.

[dict.2.astronomy]
Astronomy is the science that studies the stars, the planets and everything else in space.

[dict.2.atmosphere]
The atmosphere is the air all around the earth.

[dict.2.atmospheric]
Atmospheric means of the air.

[dict.2.atom]
An atom is a very small part of a thing.

[dict.2.atomic]
Atomic means about atoms, or as small as atoms.

[dict.2.attach]
To attach one thing to another is to join or fix them together.

[dict.2.attack]
To attack is to try to hurt, and an attack is that try.

[dict.2.attention]
Attention is looking at one thing and not at the others.

[dict.2.attic]
An attic is the room at the top of a house.

[dict.2.attract]
To attract is to pull a thing toward you.

[dict.2.attractive]
Attractive means pulling things toward each other, as a magnet pulls iron.

[dict.2.auntie]
Auntie is a name a child uses for an aunt.

[dict.2.aurora]
An aurora is a show of bright light in many colors that glows in the night sky near the north and south poles.

[dict.2.author]
An author is the person who wrote a thing.

[dict.2.automatically]
Automatically means by itself, without a person having to do it.

[dict.2.automobile]
An automobile is a car.

[dict.2.autumn]
The time of year when leaves fall from trees and the air gets cool.

[dict.2.availability]
Availability is how easy a thing is to get or to use.

[dict.2.available]
Available means here and able to be used.

[dict.2.average]

To check two counts against each other until they agree.

[dict.2.regional]

[dict.2.regularly]

[dict.2.relatively]

[dict.2.safety]

[dict.2.strange]
Strange means different from what you know or expect.

[dict.2.stratosphere]
The stratosphere is a high layer of air, above the air where weather happens.

[dict.2.stratus]
Stratus is a low flat gray cloud covering the sky.

A strawberry is a soft, sweet, red fruit with tiny seeds on its skin.

[dict.2.streak]
A streak is a long, thin line or mark.

[dict.2.streambed]
A streambed is the ground at the bottom of a stream.

[dict.2.strength]
Strength is how much a thing can push or pull.

[dict.2.strengthen]
To strengthen something is to make it stronger.

[dict.2.stretch]
To stretch something is to pull it so that it gets longer or wider.

[dict.2.strict]
Strict means following rules closely and not letting them be broken.

[dict.2.strike]
To strike something is to hit it hard.

[dict.2.struggle]

[dict.2.strut]

[dict.2.student]

[dict.2.sturdy]
Sturdy means strong and not easily broken.

[dict.2.subject]
A subject is what a book or a talk is about.

[dict.2.submarine]
A submarine is a boat that travels under the water.

[dict.2.submerge]
To submerge something is to put it under water.

[dict.2.submit]

[dict.2.subsection]
A subsection is one of the smaller parts that a section is divided into.

[dict.2.subsequent]
Subsequent means coming after.

[dict.2.substance]

[dict.2.substantial]
Substantial means large in amount.

[dict.2.substantially]
Substantially means by a large amount.

[dict.2.subsurface]

[dict.2.subtract]
To subtract is to take one amount away from another.

[dict.2.subtropical]
Subtropical means of the regions just outside the hot middle of the earth.

[dict.2.success]
Success is doing what you set out to do.

[dict.2.successful]
Successful means having done what was set out.

[dict.2.sudden]
Something sudden happens quickly when you do not expect it.

[dict.2.suddenly]
Suddenly means quickly and when you did not expect it.

[dict.2.sufficient]
Sufficient means enough.

[dict.2.sugar]
Sugar is a sweet white or brown powder, made from some plants, that people add to food and drink.

[dict.2.sulfur]
Sulfur is a yellow material that smells bad when it burns.

[dict.2.sum]
A sum is the number you get when you add numbers together.

[dict.2.summary]
A summary is a short account that gives the main points of something longer.

[dict.2.summer]
Summer is the hot part of the year.

[dict.2.summertime]
Summertime is the summer part of the year.

[dict.2.sunburn]
A sunburn is a burn of the skin from too much sun.

[dict.2.sundial]
A sundial is a tool that shows the time by the shadow the sun makes.

[dict.2.sunlight]

[dict.2.sunrise]

[dict.2.sunset]

[dict.2.sunshine]
Sunshine is bright light from the sun.

[dict.2.sunspot]

[dict.2.supercell]

[dict.2.supercooled]

[dict.2.superhighway]

[dict.2.supper]

[dict.2.supplier]

[dict.2.support]

[dict.2.surely]
Surely means without doubt.

[dict.2.surf]

[dict.2.surge]

[dict.2.surprise]

[dict.2.surroundings]
Your surroundings are the things and places all around you.

[dict.2.survey]
A survey is a careful study made to find out about a place or a group.

[dict.2.survive]
To survive is to stay alive, or to keep going through something hard.

[dict.2.sustain]
To sustain something is to keep it going over time.

[dict.2.swamp]

[dict.2.swear]

[dict.2.sweat]

[dict.2.sweet]

[dict.2.swell]

[dict.2.swiftly]
Swiftly means quickly.

[dict.2.swimmer]

[dict.2.swirl]

[dict.2.switchgrass]

[dict.2.syllable]

[dict.2.synthetic]
Synthetic means made by people and not found in nature.

[dict.2.system]
A system is many parts that work together as one thing.

[dict.2.tabby]
A tabby is a cat with dark lines in its coat.

[dict.2.tablet]

[dict.2.tan]
Tan means of a light brown color.

[dict.2.tank]

[dict.2.tape]
Tape is a long thin piece used to hold things.

[dict.2.target]
A target is what you aim at.

[dict.2.tart]
Tart means a little sour.

[dict.2.taste]
What you feel with your tongue when food or drink touches it, apart from how hot, cold, hard or soft it is.

[dict.2.tasteless]
Tasteless means with no taste.

[dict.2.tax]
A tax is money that people must pay to the government.

[dict.2.technical]
Technical means needing special knowledge of how a thing works.

[dict.2.technician]
A technician is a person whose job is to work with tools, machines or tests and keep them working well.

[dict.2.technique]
A technique is a way of doing something that has to be learned.

[dict.2.technology]
Technology is the tools and machines that people make to help them do things.

[dict.2.telephone]
A telephone is a machine used to talk to someone far away.

[dict.2.telescope]

[dict.2.television]
A television is a box that shows moving pictures and plays sound sent from far away.

[dict.2.temperature]
The temperature of something is how hot or cold it is.

[dict.2.tempest]
A tempest is a violent storm.

[dict.2.temple]
A temple is a building where people meet to practice their faith.

[dict.2.temporarily]
Temporarily means for a short time only.

[dict.2.temporary]
Temporary means lasting only for a short time.

[dict.2.temptation]
A temptation is a pull toward a thing one should not do.

[dict.2.tendency]
A tendency is a way something usually happens or acts.

[dict.2.tender]
Tender means soft, or easy to hurt.

[dict.2.tennis]
Tennis is a game of hitting a ball from one side to the other.

[dict.2.tension]

[dict.2.tent]
A tent is a small house made of cloth held up by poles, which people can sleep in outside.

[dict.2.terrain]

[dict.2.terrible]
Terrible means very bad.

[dict.2.territory]
A territory is an area of land that belongs to a country or a group, or is ruled by it.

[dict.2.terror]

[dict.2.testimony]
Testimony is what a person tells about what that person saw or did.

[dict.2.texture]
Texture is how the surface of something feels, such as rough or smooth.

[dict.2.textured]
Textured means having a surface that is not smooth, so that you can feel its shape with your fingers.

[dict.2.thaw]
To thaw is to make a frozen thing warm until it is soft.

[dict.2.theater]
A theater is a building where people go to see a play.

[dict.2.themselves]

[dict.2.thereby]

[dict.2.thermal]

[dict.2.thermocline]

[dict.2.thermosphere]

[dict.2.thickness]
Thickness is how thick a thing is.

[dict.2.thirty]
Thirty is three tens.

[dict.2.thread]

[dict.2.threat]
A threat is a warning that something bad may be done.

[dict.2.threaten]

[dict.2.thrive]
To thrive is to grow well.

[dict.2.throat]

[dict.2.throughout]
Throughout means in every part, or during all of a time.

[dict.2.thrush]

Thunder is the loud sound that follows lightning.

[dict.2.tidings]

[dict.2.tiger]

[dict.2.till]

[dict.2.tilt]

[dict.2.timely]

[dict.2.tint]

[dict.2.title]

[dict.2.toad]

[dict.2.ton]
A ton is a unit of weight equal to two thousand pounds.

[dict.2.tone]
A tone is the quality of a sound or of a voice.

[dict.2.tongue]
The soft part inside your mouth that you can move to help you eat and speak.

[dict.2.tooth]
A tooth is one of the hard white parts in the mouth used to cut and break up food.

[dict.2.toothpick]
A toothpick is a small, thin, pointed stick of wood that people use to hold bits of food together or to clean their mouth.

[dict.2.topography]

[dict.2.totally]

Towering means very tall.

Toxic means like a poison, able to harm or kill a living thing.

[dict.2.traffic]

[dict.2.transpiration]

[dict.2.trap]

[dict.2.trash]

[dict.2.triumph]

[dict.2.truly]

Turbid means not clear, because small bits are in it.

[dict.2.turbulence]

[dict.2.unhappy]

[dict.2.unhealthy]

Unique means the only one of its kind.

[dict.2.unnamed]

[dict.2.unpredictable]

[dict.2.unsightly]
Unsightly means ugly to look at.

[dict.2.unstable]

[dict.2.unusual]
Unusual means not usual, or different from what is common.

[dict.2.unusually]

[dict.2.unwanted]

[dict.2.upward]

[dict.2.usage]

[dict.2.useful]

[dict.2.user]

Usual means happening most of the time.

[dict.2.usually]

A utility is a company that sells power, water or gas to homes.

[dict.2.utter]

[dict.2.valley]

[dict.2.value]

A variation is a change from what is usual.

[dict.2.variety]

Various means of many different kinds.

[dict.2.vary]
To vary is to change, or to be different.

[dict.2.vast]

A veggie is a vegetable.

[dict.2.versus]

[dict.2.vertically]

[dict.2.vessel]

[dict.2.via]

[dict.2.vicinity]

[dict.2.victory]
A victory is a win.

[dict.2.violent]
Violent means using great force to hurt.

[dict.2.violet]

Visible means able to be seen.

To visit a person is to go see that person.

[dict.2.visual]

[dict.2.wanderer]

[dict.2.warehouse]

[dict.2.warmth]

[dict.2.warn]

[dict.2.watery]

A web is what a small animal makes to catch food.

[dict.2.website]
A website is a set of pages on a computer that other people can read.

[dict.2.wedding]
A wedding is the time when two people join together as a family, with their friends around them.

[dict.2.weed]
A weed is a wild plant that grows where it is not wanted.

[dict.2.well.liquid]
A well is a deep hole dug in the ground to get water.

[dict.2.west]
West is the direction where the sun goes down in the evening.

[dict.2.western]
Western means of the west.

[dict.2.westward]
Westward means toward the west.

[dict.2.wetland]
A wetland is land that stays wet, like a marsh.

[dict.2.wetness]
Wetness is how wet a thing is.

[dict.2.whale]
A whale is a very large animal that lives in the sea and breathes air.

[dict.2.whatever]
Whatever means any thing of the kind that is spoken of.

[dict.2.wheat]
Wheat is a grain used to make flour and bread.

[dict.2.whenever]
Whenever means at any time that, or every time that.

[dict.2.whereas]
Whereas is a word used to show how two things are different.

[dict.2.wherever]
Wherever means in any place.

[dict.2.whether]
Whether means if, when there are two things to choose from, such as yes or no.

[dict.2.whip]
A whip is a long rope on a handle, used to hit animals to make them move.

[dict.2.whipcord]
Whipcord is thin hard rope.

[dict.2.whisker]
A whisker is one of the long, thick hairs near the mouth of a cat or other animal.

[dict.2.whistle]
A whistle is a high sound made by blowing air through a small hole, or a small thing you blow to make that sound.

[dict.2.wicked]
Wicked means very bad on purpose.

[dict.2.widely]
Widely means over a wide area, or by many people.

[dict.2.widespread]
Widespread means found over a wide area.

[dict.2.widow]
A widow is a woman whose man has died.

[dict.2.width]
Width is how wide a thing is.

[dict.2.wife]
A wife is a woman a man has joined with.

[dict.2.wild]
Wild animals and plants live and grow on their own, not cared for by people.

[dict.2.wildlife]
Wildlife is wild animals and plants.

[dict.2.willing]
Willing means ready to do a thing.

[dict.2.windborne]
Windborne means carried by the wind.

[dict.2.windmill]
A windmill is a machine with blades that the wind turns to make power or pump water.

[dict.2.winter]
Winter is the cold part of the year.

[dict.2.wisdom]
Wisdom is knowing what is good to do.

[dict.2.wise]
Wise means knowing a lot and using it to choose well.

[dict.2.wish]
To wish is to want something very much, often something that may not happen.

[dict.2.withdraw]
To withdraw something is to take it out or away, such as water from a river.

[dict.2.withdrawal]
A withdrawal is the taking out of something, such as water from a river or a well.

[dict.2.within]
Within means inside something, or not past it.

[dict.2.witness]
A witness is a person who saw something, and to witness is to see it.

[dict.2.wolf]
A wolf is a wild animal that lives and catches food with others.

[dict.2.wonderful]
Wonderful means so good that it causes wonder.

[dict.2.wool]
Wool is the soft hair of a sheep, used to make cloth.

[dict.2.workman]
A workman is a man who does work with his hands.

[dict.2.worldwide]
Worldwide means over the whole world.

[dict.2.worry]
To worry is to feel afraid that something bad might happen.

[dict.2.worth]
If something is worth an amount, that is how much it is wanted or what it would cost.

[dict.2.worthy]
Worthy means good enough to be given respect, praise or attention.

[dict.2.wrap]
To wrap something is to cover it by folding cloth or paper around it.

[dict.2.wreck]
A wreck is what is left when a thing is broken.

[dict.2.wrench]
A wrench is a tool used to hold and turn nuts and pipes.

[dict.2.writer]
A writer is a person who writes books, stories or other words.

[dict.2.yearly]
Yearly means happening once a year or every year.

[dict.2.yeast]
Yeast is a tiny living thing put into dough to make it rise, so that bread comes out soft when it is baked.

[dict.2.yield]
To yield something is to give it or make it, such as water or crops.

[dict.2.youth]
Youth is the time of being young.

[dict.2.zinc]
Zinc is a gray metal used to cover steel so that it does not rust.

[dict.2.zonal]
Zonal means lying along an east and west line.

[dict.2.zone]
A zone is one part of a place and not the other parts.

[dict.2.zooplankton]
Zooplankton is the very small animals that float in the water.
